Base 40, validation 12 and trailing audit

Retrospective 20-symbol Low-Range Short research · 1/5/15-minute candles · modeled cost 15 bp per round trip

What was measured

The production Base recent-close gate was applied to 3,900 candidate cells. 45 passed Base; 25/90 direction contexts (27.78%) validated on 2026-10-09 12:00:00 UTC using the preceding 24 hours, with historical pooled return PF 2.892. In the separate next 24 hours, the walk-forward selector produced 37 modeled closes at PF 0.964. Positions opened / closed / held: 17 / 17 / 0; orders opened / settled / held: 37 / 37 / 0.

This is a bounded research screen feeding the actual selector, not a full runtime deployment, a fresh holdout, funded venue fills, or a promise of future PF. The 74 candidate-screen closes at PF 0.806 include orders the selector did not execute and must not be mixed with the 37 modeled executions below. Candle bars cannot establish the exact intrabar path.
